Continuing work from thread T-019c7065-499e-7489-a60e-dc4beea24ac2. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@src/bin/screenshot.rs @screenshots/scenarios/csv.yaml @src/syntax/mod.rs @src/syntax/parser.rs @src/model/document.rs @src/csv/mod.rs @screenshots/scenarios/hero.yaml

Other relevant files: src/model/editor.rs, src/view/mod.rs

- I built a headless screenshot pipeline that renders Token editor to PNG using the real Renderer code path. The binary is at src/bin/screenshot.rs and uses YAML scenario files from screenshots/scenarios/.

- The screenshots currently lack syntax highlighting. The rendering uses the real Renderer static methods (render_editor_area, render_status_bar, render_splitters) which do render syntax tokens, but the documents created in the screenshot binary don't have syntax highlighting applied. The Document::with_text() call doesn't trigger syntax parsing. In the normal editor flow, syntax parsing is scheduled via schedule_syntax_parse() after opening a file, which populates document.syntax_highlights. The screenshot binary needs to run syntax highlighting on each document after creating it so the tokens are available during rendering.

- For the CSV scenario, I want to showcase the CSV viewer mode (the tabular grid view), not just raw CSV text. The editor has a CSV view mode (editor.view_mode) that renders a spreadsheet-like grid. The screenshot binary currently opens CSV files as plain text. It needs to detect or be told (via scenario YAML) to switch to CSV view mode.

- Minor UX fix: the output paths printed by the screenshot binary should use "./" prefix (e.g. "./website/v4/public/screenshot-hero.png") so the entire line is clickable in the terminal.

- The view module was moved from main.rs to lib.rs as pub mod view, and the perf module was extracted to src/perf.rs. Renderer static methods are pub.

- Syntax highlighting is done via token::syntax module. Look at how the normal editor applies syntax - likely Document has a syntax_highlights field populated by the syntax parser. The syntax parser uses Tree-sitter.

- CSV view mode is in token::csv module. EditorState has a view_mode field. Check how CSV mode is toggled in the normal editor flow.

- The scenario YAML schema is defined inline in screenshot.rs with Scenario, ScenarioFile, SplitDir structs. It could be extended with a view_mode field for CSV.

- The output line is currently: eprintln!(" saved {}", out_path.display()) - just needs "./" prefix when the path doesn't start with "/" or ".".

`ViewMode` is an enum defined in `src/model/editor.rs` that allows an editor to switch between standard text and specialized views like CSV.

### ViewMode Definition
The `ViewMode` enum and its `Csv` variant (which holds a `CsvState`) are defined in:
- [src/model/editor.rs#L273-L279](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/editor.rs#L273-L279)

### Activation and Toggling
CSV mode is activated through a toggle mechanism:
1.  **Command**: The `ToggleCsvView` command is defined in the command palette.
    - [src/commands.rs#L222-L225](file://~/code/token-editor/src/commands.rs#L222-L225)
2.  **Message**: The command maps to `CsvMsg::Toggle`.
    - [src/messages.rs#L422-L424](file://~/code/token-editor/src/messages.rs#L422-L424)
3.  **Update Logic**: The `toggle_csv_mode` function in `src/update/csv.rs` handles the transition. It parses the current document content using a detected delimiter and updates the editor's `view_mode` to `ViewMode::Csv(Box::new(csv_state))`.
    - [src/update/csv.rs#L70-L100](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/csv.rs#L70-L100)
4.  **App Dispatch**: The main app update loop routes the command to the CSV update module.
    - [src/update/app.rs#L289](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/app.rs#L289)

**Tool Result:** `toolu_vrtx_0154EiSrubtu49TizqzZcaVG`

```
The syntax highlighting flow follows a debounced, asynchronous model within an Elm-style architecture:

1.  **Trigger**: When a document is opened (`open_file_in_new_tab`) or edited (`DocumentMsg`), a call to `schedule_syntax_parse` is made.
    *   [src/update/layout.rs#L280-L286](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/layout.rs#L280-L286)
    *   [src/update/syntax.rs#L191-L219](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/syntax.rs#L191-L219)
2.  **Debounce**: `schedule_syntax_parse` returns `Cmd::DebouncedSyntaxParse`. After 30ms, the runtime sends a `SyntaxMsg::ParseReady` back to the update loop.
3.  **Request**: `update_syntax` handles `ParseReady` by snapshotting the document's `Rope` buffer into a `String` and returning `Cmd::RunSyntaxParse`.
    *   [src/update/syntax.rs#L18-L81](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/syntax.rs#L18-L81)
4.  **Worker Thread**: The runtime catches `Cmd::RunSyntaxParse`, sends it to a background worker thread (`syntax_worker_loop`), which calls `ParserState::parse_and_highlight`.
    *   [src/runtime/app.rs#L1453-L1475](file://~/code/token-editor/src/runtime/app.rs#L1453-L1475)
5.  **Parsing**: `ParserState` uses Tree-sitter (supporting incremental parsing via a `doc_cache`) to generate `SyntaxHighlights`.
    *   [src/syntax/parser.rs#L280-L310](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/parser.rs#L280-L310)
6.  **Application**: The worker thread sends a `SyntaxMsg::ParseCompleted` message. The `update_syntax` handler receives this and populates `Document.syntax_highlights`.
    *   [src/update/syntax.rs#L83-L153](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/syntax.rs#L83-L153)

Relevant files:
- [src/update/syntax.rs#L16-L219](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/syntax.rs#L16-L219) (Update logic & scheduling)
- [src/update/layout.rs#L204-L286](file://~/code/token-editor/src/update/layout.rs#L204-L286) (Initial trigger on file open)
- [src/runtime/app.rs#L1453-L1490](file://~/code/token-editor/src/runtime/app.rs#L1453-L1490) (Background worker execution)
- [src/syntax/parser.rs#L280-L350](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/parser.rs#L280-L350) (Tree-sitter integration)
- [src/model/document.rs#L60-L75](file://~/code/token-editor/src/model/document.rs#L60-L75) (Document state definition)

Images, CSS, and JS files are now served from the document's directory via a custom `token://` protocol handler.\n16: - Preview: Preview now updates to show the new file when switching tabs (if the new file supports preview), instead of closing.\n17: - API: Added `LanguageId::supports_preview()` method to check if a file type supports live preview.\n18: - API: Added `content_to_preview_html()` function that handles both Markdown and HTML content.\n19: \n20: ---\n21: \n22: ## v0.3.14 - 2026-01-09\n23: \n24: ### Added\n25: - Docs: Updated developer OVERVIEW and archived obsolete docs to reflect recent refactors and module moves.\n26: - Tests: Comprehensive damage computation tests added for redraw/damage logic.\n27: \n28: ### Fixed\n29: - Layout: Editor viewport now correctly shrinks to accommodate dock panels (bottom/right). Fixed issue where dock panels would obscure part of the editor area because the logical viewport didn't account for dock sizes.\n30: - Layout: Viewport dimensions now recalculated when dock visibility or size changes.\n31: - Preview: Fixed dual rendering issue where both native markdown and webview were drawn. Native rendering is now properly disabled when webview is active.\n32: - Preview: Fixed webview misalignment by correctly converting physical pixel coordinates to logical points with proper Y-axis flipping for macOS.\n33: - Scroll: Scroll events over dock panels and preview panes are now properly consumed instead of bleeding into the editor.\n34: - Rendering: Minor fixes to selection/cursor rendering behavior related to the cursor-blink optimization and partial redraws.\n35: - Sidebar: Fixed syntax highlighting not triggering when opening files via sidebar double-click. Commands from update() calls in mouse handlers are now properly propagated.\n36: \n37: ### Changed\n38: - Refactor (mouse): Unified mouse event handling with new hit-test system in `src/view/hit_test.rs` and centralized dispatch in `src/runtime/mouse.rs`. Replaces ad-hoc if/else chains with explicit `HitTarget` enum and priority-ordered hit-testing.\n39: - Refactor (app): Introduced specific redraw helpers and tighter damage accumulation to reduce unnecessary full redraws; clearer separation between damage computation and command processing.\n40: - Refactor (layout): Added `redraw_editor`-style helpers to limit redraw scope when layout changes are localized.\n41: - Refactor (ui): Optimized cursor-blink related redraw behavior to prevent selection flicker and avoid unnecessary redraws of unrelated regions.\n42: - Cleanup: Removed dead code from view module (`is_in_tab_bar`, `is_in_modal`, unused Renderer getters, wrapper functions superseded by ViewportGeometry).\n43: \n44: ---\n45: \n46: ## v0.3.13 - 2026-01-07\n47: \n48: ### Fixed - CSV Cell Editing\n49: \n50: **Column Truncation:**\n51: - Fixed column truncation issue during CSV cell editing\n52: - Cells now properly handle content that exceeds initial column width\n53: - No data loss when editing longer values\n54: \n55: **Horizontal Scrolling:**\n56: - Added horizontal scroll support for CSV cell editing\n57: - Long cell content now scrollable within the edit field\n58: - Improved UX for editing cells with long text values\n59: \n60: **Log File Discovery:**\n61: - Fixed log file discovery to find most recent dated log file\n62: - Improved config path resolution for logging\n63: \n64: ### Added - Command Palette Enhancements\n65: \n66: **New Commands:**\n67: - Added \"Open Folder...\" command to open workspace directories\n68: - Added \"Quit\" command (⌘Q) to close the application\n69: - Added \"Toggle Performance Overlay\" command (F2) - debug builds only\n70: - Added \"Toggle Debug Overlay\" command (F8) - debug builds only\n71: \n72: **Implementation:**\n73: - Added `Cmd::Quit` for proper application exit flow\n74: - Debug commands use conditional compilation (`#[cfg(debug_assertions)]`)\n75: - Added `DEBUG_COMMANDS` array for debug-only command definitions\n76: \n77: ### Added - Configuration and Command System\n78: \n79: **Configuration Reload:**\n80: - Implemented `ReloadConfiguration` command for hot-reloading config without restart\n[... omitted lines 81 to 1545 ...]",

```
### Original Goal of the Screenshot Pipeline
The primary goal was to build a headless screenshot binary that renders the Token editor in configurable scenarios to PNG files. These real screenshots were intended to replace manual HTML/CSS "fake" editors on the website to ensure the visual representation of the editor never diverged from the actual rendering code path.

### Website Structure and Design
The website is an Astro-based site located at `website/v4/`. Key details include:
- **Design System:** Uses a "Fleet Dark" inspired theme with design tokens defined in `website/v4/src/styles/tokens.css`.
- **Homepage Sections:**
  - **Hero:** A two-column grid featuring text and a visual of the editor.
  - **Stats:** Displays metrics like language support (20+), startup time (<50ms), and "0 Electron."
  - **Features:** Grid of feature cards (e.g., multi-cursor, split views).
  - **Showcase:** A section dedicated to a large screenshot of the editor.
  - **Other Sections:** Languages marquee, download section, keybindings preview (CSV-style table), and an AI section.

### Usage of Screenshots on the Website
- **Hero Visual:** The HTML-based "fake editor" was replaced with a real screenshot (`screenshot-hero.png`) wrapped in a custom `.editor-window-chrome` container with macOS-style window controls (red/yellow/green dots).
- **Showcase Section:** Updated to use `screenshot-splits.png` to demonstrate the editor's multi-pane capabilities.
- **Automation:** A `make screenshots` target was added to the root Makefile to generate all website assets and output them directly to `website/v4/public/`.
- **Scenarios:** Specific YAML files were created to generate screenshots for different use cases: `hero`, `multi-cursor`, `splits`, `csv`, and `minimal`.

### Planned Future Improvements
The following items were identified as future work beyond the initial implementation:
- **Visual Regression Testing:** Adding a `--diff` mode to compare newly generated screenshots against "golden images."
- **Sidebar Rendering:** Implementing sidebar (file tree) rendering in screenshots, which requires setting up a mock workspace.
- **CSV Mode:** Forcing CSV view mode within scenario configurations.
- **Animations:** Generating animated GIFs to demonstrate multi-cursor editing dynamically.
- **Platform Chrome:** Adding per-platform window chrome (macOS, Linux, and Windows title bars) to the generated images.

Skills used: subagent-driven-development
```

if a topic needs more exploration, break it into multiple questions\n- Focus on understanding: purpose, constraints, success criteria\n\n**Exploring approaches:**\n- Propose 2-3 different approaches with trade-offs\n- Present options conversationally with your recommendation and reasoning\n- Lead with your recommended option and explain why\n\n**Presenting the design:**\n- Once you believe you understand what you're building, present the design\n- Break it into sections of 200-300 words\n- Ask after each section whether it looks right so far\n- Cover: architecture, components, data flow, error handling, testing\n- Be ready to go back and clarify if something doesn't make sense\n\n## After the Design\n\n**Documentation:**\n- Write the validated design to `docs/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<topic>-design.md`\n- Use elements-of-style:writing-clearly-and-concisely skill if available\n- Commit the design document to git\n\n**Implementation (if continuing):**\n- Ask: \"Ready to set up for implementation?\"\n- Use superpowers:using-git-worktrees to create isolated workspace\n- Use superpowers:writing-plans to create detailed implementation plan\n\n## Key Principles\n\n- **One question at a time** - ```
### Visual Analysis for Bento Grid Scaling

When scaled to a ~600px width, this screenshot will encounter the following issues:

*   **Empty Space:** There is excessive empty space on the right side of the image. The code occupies approximately 40% of the width, while the remaining 60% is a solid black void.
*   **Chrome Dominance:** At 600px, the tab bar (top) and status bar (bottom) will appear as thin, illegible strips of text. They consume vertical space that could be used to make the code larger and more readable.
*   **Gutter Impact:** The line number gutter further compresses the actual code content toward the center-left, making the functional part of the image even smaller.
*   **Aspect Ratio:** The image has an approximate **16:10** aspect ratio.

### Recommendations for Tighter Crop
To improve visual impact at small scales:
1.  **Crop the Right Side:** Remove the empty space to the right of the code (around column 60-80).
2.  **Remove UI Chrome:** Strip the tab bar and status bar.
3.  **Focus Content:** Crop vertically to focus on the `Person` struct and the `new` function (lines 8–24). This allows the text to be significantly larger within the 600px container.
```

**Tool Result:** `toolu_vrtx_01YH645PH9qJjsHi79ELc3rA`

```
### Evaluation for 300px Wide Bento Card

#### Legibility
*   **Poor.** At a width of 300px, each code pane is roughly 100px wide. The text will be reduced to illegible colored lines ("greeking").
*   The three-column layout is too dense for small containers; users will see the *pattern* of code rather than the content.

#### UI Chrome
*   **Excessive.** The screenshot includes repeating elements that consume valuable horizontal space:
    *   **Line Numbers:** Each pane has a gutter (e.g., `sample.rs` lines 1-46).
    *   **Headers:** Filenames are repeated at the top.
    *   **Status Bar:** The bottom bar (`sample.ts`, `Ln 1, Col 1`) adds vertical bulk without providing value in a small card.
*   The chrome-to-content ratio is high, further squeezing the actual code.

#### Aspect Ratio
*   **Approximately 1.8:1 (Wide).** The image is significantly wider than it is tall.
*   In a standard square or portrait-oriented bento card, this will result in large letterboxing (empty space) above and below the image, or require extreme scaling that further ruins legibility.

#### Summary
*   **Verdict:** Does not work for a 300px card if the goal is readability.
*   **Recommendation:** Use a single-pane screenshot or a cropped version focusing on a specific code block (e.g., lines 10-20 of one file) and remove line numbers/status bars to maximize the font size.
```

**Cursor Visibility**

*   They are distinct but small; their visibility depends on the final scaling of the bento card.

**Positioning**
*   The screenshot currently starts at line 16.
*   The cursors (lines 20-22) are located in the top third of the frame.
*   **Effect of scrolling to line 15:** This would shift the cursors down by one line, keeping them in a "top-center" position relative to the vertical axis, which aligns with the stated objective.

**Empty Space**

**Tool Result:** `toolu_vrtx_01NGnJvDHU7qC9ym7kjKCPyt`

```
### Readability
The code is highly readable. The font size and line spacing are well-balanced for the 1400x1200 resolution. Syntax highlighting provides clear distinction between keywords, types, and strings.

### Comparison to 3-pane Layout
A 2-pane layout is superior for small card sizes:
- **Horizontal Space:** Each pane has ~700px width, preventing premature line wrapping or horizontal scrolling that would occur in a 3-pane (~466px) configuration.
- **Indentation:** The Rust `impl` block (lines 16-36) and the TS `User` interface (lines 13-20) maintain clear indentation levels without crowding the right margin.

### Pane Utilization
Both panes are effectively utilized:
- **`sample.rs` (Left):** Displays a complete data structure and its logic (`struct` and `impl`). It highlights Rust-specific features like `Option<T>`, `impl Into<String>`, and `mut self`.
- **`sample.ts` (Right):** Showcases TypeScript's type system including interfaces, generics (`ApiResponse<T>`), inheritance (`extends`), and string enums.
- **Balance:** Both files show approximately 25-28 lines of code, creating visual symmetry. No excessive whitespace is present.
```

### Analysis of `large_data.csv` (1400x1200)

**Space Utilization**
The CSV grid fills the space effectively. It displays 27 data rows and 6 columns (A through F), utilizing nearly the entire vertical and horizontal canvas. There is minimal dead space, primarily a small section at the bottom-right.

**Visibility**
*   **Column Headers:** The Excel-style headers (A, B, C, D, E, F) and field names (`id`, `first_name`, `last_name`, `email`, `company`, `department`) are highly legible.
*   **Row Data:** Individual cell values are sharp. Long strings, such as emails in Column D (e.g., `Carlos.Rodriguez@company.com` at row 21), are fully visible without truncation in this specific view.
*   **UI Elements:** The status bar at the bottom (`Ln 1, Col 1`) and the filename tab are clear.

**Comparison to Full-Size CSV Screenshots**
*   **Density:** At this 1-column bento card size, the grid maintains a "high-density" professional look without the text becoming illegible, which often happens when full-size (e.g., 4K) spreadsheets are scaled down.
*   **Information Volume:** It shows enough data (27 rows) to convey the "large data" theme effectively, whereas a smaller crop might look like a simple table rather than a robust CSV editor.
*   **Clarity:** The 1400x1200 resolution ensures that the 1px grid lines and subtle text contrast remain crisp, which is typically lost in lower-resolution previews.

### Website Configuration and Structure
The website is an Astro project located in `website/v4/`. It uses a design system inspired by the "Fleet Dark" theme.

#### Relevant Files and Paths
- **Layout**: `website/v4/src/layouts/SiteLayout.astro` (Imported in pages, but full content not displayed in thread).
- **Styles**: `website/v4/src/styles/tokens.css` (Contains design tokens for colors and surfaces).
- **Pages**:
  - `website/v4/src/pages/index.astro`: The main homepage.
  - `website/v4/src/pages/keybindings.astro`: A redesigned page featuring a CSV-style table view.
  - `website/v4/src/pages/keybindings.old.astro`: The previous version of the keybindings page.
  - `website/v4/src/pages/themes.astro`: Mentioned in build logs.
- **Assets**: Generated screenshots are stored in `website/v4/public/`.

**Hero Section (Updated with Real Screenshot and Trust Badges):**
```html
<section class="hero">
  <div class="hero-text">
    <p class="hero-tag">Text editor / Rust / MIT</p>
    <h1>
      <span>Multi-cursor.</span>
      <span>Syntax aware.</span>
      <span class="accent">Absurdly fast.</span>
    </h1>
    <p class="hero-desc">Token is a minimal code editor with multi-cursor editing, split views, and Tree-sitter syntax highlighting. No Electron. No bloat. Just code.</p>
    <div class="hero-actions">
      <a href="#download" class="btn-primary">Download <span class="arrow">↓</span></a>
      <a href="https://github.com/HelgeSverre/token" class="btn-ghost" target="_blank">View source →</a>
    </div>
    <div class="hero-trust">
      <span class="trust-item">MIT Licensed</span>
      <span class="trust-sep">·</span>
      <span class="trust-item">No Telemetry</span>
      <span class="trust-sep">·</span>
      <span class="trust-item">Open Source</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="hero-editor">
    <div class="editor-frame">
      <div class="editor-window-chrome">
        <span class="dot red"></span>
        <span class="dot yellow"></span>
        <span class="dot green"></span>
      </div>
      <img src="/screenshot-hero.png" alt="Token editor showing Rust code with multi-cursor editing and split views" loading="eager" />
    </div>
  </div>
</section>
```

**Showcase Section:**
```html
<section class="showcase">
  <div class="editor-frame showcase-frame">
    <div class="editor-window-chrome">
      <span class="dot red"></span>
      <span class="dot yellow"></span>
      <span class="dot green"></span>
    </div>
    <img src="/screenshot-splits.png" alt="Token editor with three split views" />

</section>

**CSS Additions for v4 Improvements:**
```css
/* Window Chrome */
.editor-window-chrome {
  display: flex;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 10px 14px;
  background: var(--panel-bg);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
}
.dot {
  width: 10px;
  height: 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
}
.dot.red { background: #FF5F57; }
.dot.yellow { background: #FEBC2E; }
.dot.green { background: #28C840; }

/* Hero Interactions & Trust Badges */
.hero-editor .editor-frame {
  transition: box-shadow var(--duration-normal) var(--ease-out);
}
.hero-editor .editor-frame:hover {
  box-shadow: 0 25px 60px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.5),
              0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04),
              var(--shadow-glow);
}

.hero-trust {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-top: 24px;
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  color: var(--fg-muted);
}
.trust-sep {
  opacity: 0.4;
}

/* Micro-interactions */
.feature-card {
  padding: 40px;
  background: var(--panel-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--r-lg);
  transition: border-color 0.2s, transform 0.2s var(--ease-out);
}
.feature-card:hover {
  border-color: var(--border-strong);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.btn-primary .arrow {
  display: inline-block;
  transition: transform var(--duration-normal) var(--ease-out);
}
.btn-primary:hover .arrow {
  transform: translateY(3px);
}
```

You can create your own by copying <code>themes/fleet-dark.yaml</code> and modifying the colors.</p>\n</section>\n\n</SiteLayout>\n\n<style>\n.themes-page {\n  padding: 120px 48px 80px;\n  max-width: 1000px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n}\n.themes-page h1 {\n  font-size: 2.5rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  letter-spacing: -0.03em;\n  margin-bottom: 12px;\n}\n.page-desc {\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  font-size: 1.1rem;\n  margin-bottom: 48px;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n}\n.page-desc code {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  background: var(--bg-deep);\n  padding: 2px 6px;\n  border-radius: 3px;\n  color: var(--fg);\n}\n.theme-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(280px, 1fr));\n  gap: 20px;\n  margin-bottom: 48px;\n}\n.theme-card {\n  background: var(--panel-bg);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--r-lg);\n  padding: 20px;\n  transition: border-color 0.2s;\n}\n.theme-card.active {\n  border-color: var(--syn-keyword);\n}\n.theme-preview {\n  background: #181818;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--r-sm);\n  padding: 12px 16px;\n  margin-bottom: 16px;\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.75rem;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n  color: #BCBEC4;\n}\n.tp-line {\n  white-space: pre;\n}\n.theme-name {\n  display: block;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n  margin-bottom: 4px;\n}\n.theme-tag {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.65rem;\n  color: var(--syn-string);\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.1em;\n}\n.themes-note {\n  color: var(--fg-muted);\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n  line-height: 1.7;\n}\n.themes-note code {\n  font-family: var(--font-mono);\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\n  background: var(--bg-deep);\n  padding: 2px 6px;\n  border-radius: 3px;\n  color: var(--fg);\n}\n@media (max-width: 768px) {\n  .themes-page { padding: 100px 24px 60px; }\n  .themes-page h1 { font-size: 2rem; }\n}\n</style>\n"
}

The following files represent the core of the Token editor v4 website redesign, built with Astro and styled using the Fleet Dark color palette. Note that while some files were created by sub-agents and their full source is not directly visible in the main thread, the foundational layouts, components, and the homepage were explicitly provided or read in full. For files where only snippets or prompts are available, those are included to preserve fidelity.

### 1. website/v4/src/layouts/SiteLayout.astro
This is the base layout for the site, including the head metadata, font imports (Inter, Space Grotesk, Space Mono), and the global footer.

```astro
---
import Navbar from '../components/Navbar.astro';
import '../styles/global.css';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
}

const { title, description = 'A fast, GPU-rendered code editor built in Rust.' } = Astro.props;
---

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
    <meta name="description" content={description} />
    <meta name="generator" content={Astro.generator} />

    <link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/icon.png" />

    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
    <link
      href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@500;600;700&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Space+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap"
      rel="stylesheet"
    />

    <title>{title}</title>
  </head>
  <body>
    <Navbar />
    <main>
      <slot />
    </main>
    <footer class="site-footer">
      <div class="footer-inner">
        <div class="footer-brand">
          <span class="footer-logo">Token</span>
          <span class="footer-license">MIT License</span>
        </div>
        <div class="footer-links">
          <a href="https://github.com/helgesverre/token" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">GitHub</a>
          <a href="/keybindings">Keys</a>
          <a href="/themes">Themes</a>
          <a href="https://x.com/HelgeSverre" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">@HelgeSverre</a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  </body>
</html>

<style>
  main {
    padding-top: 48px;
  }

  .site-footer {
    border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
    padding: var(--space-8) var(--space-5);
    margin-top: var(--space-20);
  }

  .footer-inner {
    max-width: 1200px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: var(--space-4);
  }

  .footer-brand {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-3);
  }

  .footer-logo {
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: var(--text-sm);
    color: var(--syn-function);
  }

  .footer-license {
    font-size: var(--text-xs);
    color: var(--fg-muted);
  }

  .footer-links {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--space-5);
  }

  .footer-links a {
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-size: var(--text-xs);
    color: var(--fg-muted);
    text-decoration: none;
    transition: color var(--duration-fast) var(--ease-out);
  }

  .footer-links a:hover {
    color: var(--fg-bright);
  }

  @media (max-width: 768px) {
    .footer-inner {
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: var(--space-4);
      text-align: center;
    }
  }
</style>
```

### 2. website/v4/src/components/Navbar.astro
A sleek, editor-inspired navbar with backdrop blur and tab-style navigation.

```astro
---
const currentPath = Astro.url.pathname;

const navLinks = [
  { href: '/', label: 'Home' },
  { href: '/keybindings', label: 'Keys' },
  { href: '/themes', label: 'Themes' },
];

function isActive(href: string): boolean {
  if (href === '/') return currentPath === '/' || currentPath === '';
  return currentPath.startsWith(href);
}
---

<nav class="navbar">
  <div class="navbar-inner">
    <a href="/" class="logo">
      <span class="logo-text">Token</span>
    </a>

    <div class="nav-links">
      {navLinks.map((link) => (
        <a
          href={link.href}
          class:list={['nav-tab', { active: isActive(link.href) }]}
        >
          {link.label}
        </a>
      ))}

      <span class="separator"></span>

      <a

### 3. website/v4/src/styles/tokens.css
The design system tokens derived from the Fleet Dark theme.

```css
/* ==========================================================================
   Token Editor – Design Tokens
   Derived from themes/fleet-dark.yaml ("Fleet Dark")
   ========================================================================== */

:root {
  /* ── Editor surfaces ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --bg:          #181818;
  --bg-deep:     #121212;
  --bg-raised:   #1E1E1E;
  --bg-hover:    color-mix(in srgb, #FFFFFF 4%, #181818);
  --bg-active:   color-mix(in srgb, #FFFFFF 8%, #181818);

  --fg:          #BCBEC4;
  --fg-muted:    #606366;
  --fg-bright:   #FFFEF8;
  --fg-dim:      color-mix(in srgb, #BCBEC4 60%, transparent);

  /* ── Borders ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --border:         #2B2B2B;
  --border-subtle:  #222222;
  --border-strong:  #383838;
  --border-hover:   color-mix(in srgb, #FFFFFF 14%, #2B2B2B);

  /* ── Interactive state ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --selection:   #214283;
  --cursor:      #FFFEF8;
  --status-bg:   #1E1E1E;

  /* ── Syntax palette → site accents ────────────────────────────────────── */
  --syn-keyword:     #CC7832;
  --syn-function:    #FFC66D;
  --syn-string:      #6A8759;
  --syn-comment:     #808080;
  --syn-type:        #CC7832;
  --syn-variable:    #BCBEC4;
  --syn-number:      #6897BB;
  --syn-constant:    #6897BB;
  --syn-operator:    #A9B7C6;
  --syn-punctuation: #A9B7C6;
  --syn-tag:         #E8BF6A;
  --syn-attribute:   #BABABA;
  --syn-escape:      #CC7832;
  --syn-property:    #9876AA;

  /* ── Panels / overlays ────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --panel-bg:        #1E1E1E;
  --panel-fg:        #BCBEC4;
  --overlay-bg:      color-mix(in srgb, #181818 88%, transparent);
  --overlay-border:  #2B2B2B;

  /* ── Semantic ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --accent:          #CC7832;
  --accent-hover:    color-mix(in srgb, #CC7832 80%, #FFFFFF);
  --accent-muted:    color-mix(in srgb, #CC7832 40%, transparent);
  --success:         #6AAF6A;
  --warning:         #D9A74A;
  --error:           #E05252;

  /* ── Typography ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --font-sans: 'Space Grotesk', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --font-mono: 'Space Mono', ui-monospace, 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
  --font-btn: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;

  --text-xs:   0.75rem;    /* 12px */
  --text-sm:   0.875rem;   /* 14px */
  --text-base: 1rem;       /* 16px */
  --text-lg:   1.125rem;   /* 18px */
  --text-xl:   1.25rem;    /* 20px */
  --text-2xl:  1.5rem;     /* 24px */
  --text-3xl:  1.875rem;   /* 30px */
  --text-4xl:  2.25rem;    /* 36px */
  --text-5xl:  3rem;       /* 48px */

  --leading-tight:  1.2;
  --leading-normal: 1.6;
  --leading-loose:  1.8;

  --tracking-tight: -0.02em;
  --tracking-normal: 0;
  --tracking-wide:   0.04em;

  /* ── Spacing / Radius ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --r-sm:  4px;
  --r-md:  8px;
  --r-lg:  12px;
  --r-xl:  16px;
  --r-full: 9999px;

  --space-1:  4px;
  --space-2:  8px;
  --space-3:  12px;
  --space-4:  16px;
  --space-5:  20px;
  --space-6:  24px;
  --space-8:  32px;
  --space-10: 40px;
  --space-12: 48px;
  --space-16: 64px;
  --space-20: 80px;
  --space-24: 96px;

  /* ── Shadows ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --shadow-sm:  0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
  --shadow-md:  0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  --shadow-lg:  0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
  --shadow-xl:  0 16px 48px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
  --shadow-glow: 0 0 20px color-mix(in srgb, #CC7832 30%, transparent);

  /* ── Transitions ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --ease-out: cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
  --ease-in-out: cubic-bezier(0.65, 0, 0.35, 1);
  --duration-fast: 120ms;
  --duration-normal: 200ms;
  --duration-slow: 400ms;

  /* ── Z-index ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --z-base:    0;
  --z-raised:  10;
  --z-overlay: 100;
  --z-modal:   200;
  --z-toast:   300;
  --z-grain:   9999;
}

### 6. Data Files (Prompts)
The data files were generated based on the following structured prompts:

**src/data/keybindings.ts:**
- Export `keybindings: KeybindingCategory[]`.
- Categories: files, editing, selection, navigation, workspace.
- Items include: `action`, `keys` (using symbols like ⌘, ⌥, ⇧), `command`, `when` conditions, and `notes`.

**src/data/themes.ts:**
- Export `themes: ThemeData[]` for Default Dark, GitHub Dark, Fleet Dark, and GitHub Light.
- Includes `colors` object mapping to theme YAML values (bg, fg, syntax colors) and a `yaml` string snippet for display.

### 7. website/v4/public/install.sh
A platform-detecting shell script for one-line installation.

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Token Editor — Install Script
# Usage: curl -fsSL https://token-editor.com/install.sh | sh
set -e

REPO="HelgeSverre/token"
INSTALL_DIR="/usr/local/bin"

# ── Detect platform ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
OS="$(uname -s)"
ARCH="$(uname -m)"

case "$OS" in
  Darwin) PLATFORM="macos" ;;
  Linux)  PLATFORM="linux" ;;
  MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*) PLATFORM="windows" ;;
  *) echo "Error: unsupported OS: $OS" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac

case "$ARCH" in
  x86_64|amd64)  ARCH="x86_64" ;;
  arm64|aarch64) ARCH="aarch64" ;;
  *) echo "Error: unsupported architecture: $ARCH" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac

if [ "$PLATFORM" = "windows" ]; then
  EXT="zip"
  ASSET="token-${PLATFORM}-${ARCH}.${EXT}"
else
  EXT="tar.gz"
  ASSET="token-${PLATFORM}-${ARCH}.${EXT}"
fi

URL="https://github.com/${REPO}/releases/latest/download/${ASSET}"

# ── Print banner ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
echo ""
echo "  ┌─────────────────────────────────┐"
echo "  │  Token Editor — Install Script  │"
echo "  └─────────────────────────────────┘"
echo ""
echo "  Platform:  ${PLATFORM} (${ARCH})"
echo "  Asset:     ${ASSET}"
echo "  Install:   ${INSTALL_DIR}/token"
echo ""

# ── Download and install ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
TMPDIR="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "$TMPDIR"' EXIT

echo "  Downloading..."
if command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  curl -fsSL "$URL" -o "$TMPDIR/$ASSET"
elif command -v wget >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  wget -qO "$TMPDIR/$ASSET" "$URL"
else
  echo "Error: curl or wget required" >&2; exit 1
fi

echo "  Extracting..."
cd "$TMPDIR"
if [ "$EXT" = "zip" ]; then
  unzip -q "$ASSET"
else
  tar xzf "$ASSET"
fi

echo "  Installing to ${INSTALL_DIR}/token..."
if [ -w "$INSTALL_DIR" ]; then
  mv token "$INSTALL_DIR/token"
else
  sudo mv token "$INSTALL_DIR/token"
fi

chmod +x "$INSTALL_DIR/token"

echo ""
echo "  ✓ Token installed successfully!"
echo ""
echo "  Run 'token <file>' to get started."
echo ""
```

```astro
---
import SiteLayout from '../layouts/SiteLayout.astro';
import { keybindings } from '../data/keybindings';

const allItems = keybindings.flatMap((cat) =>
  cat.items.map((item) => ({ ...item, category: cat.label, categoryIcon: cat.icon }))
);
---

<SiteLayout title="Keybindings – Token" description="Keyboard shortcut reference for Token editor">
  <div class="kb-page" data-keybindings={JSON.stringify(keybindings)}>
    <!-- Toolbar -->
    <div class="kb-toolbar">
      <div class="kb-toolbar-left">
        <span class="kb-toolbar-title">keybindings.csv</span>
        <span class="kb-toolbar-count">{allItems.length} bindings</span>
      </div>
      <div class="kb-toolbar-right">
        <div class="kb-search-wrap">
          <svg class="kb-search-icon" width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
            <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65"/>
          </svg>
          <input type="text" class="kb-search" id="kb-search" placeholder="Search actions, keys, commands…" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" />
        </div>
        <div class="kb-view-toggle" id="kb-view-toggle">
          <button class="kb-toggle-btn active" data-view="table" title="Table view">
            <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><line x1="3" y1="9" x2="21" y2="9"/><line x1="3" y1="15" x2="21" y2="15"/><line x1="9" y1="3" x2="9" y2="21"/></svg>
          </button>
          <button class="kb-toggle-btn" data-view="yaml" title="YAML view">
            <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><polyline points="4 7 4 4 20 4 20 7"/><line x1="9" y1="20" x2="15" y2="20"/><line x1="12" y1="4" x2="12" y2="20"/></svg>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Table View -->
    <div class="kb-table-wrap" id="kb-table-view">
      <table class="kb-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th class="kb-th kb-th-row">#</th>
            <th class="kb-th kb-th-cat">Category</th>
            <th class="kb-th kb-th-action">Action</th>
            <th class="kb-th kb-th-keys">Keys</th>
            <th class="kb-th kb-th-command">Command</th>
            <th class="kb-th kb-th-when">When</th>
            <th class="kb-th kb-th-notes">Notes</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="kb-tbody">
          {allItems.map((item, i) => (
            <tr class="kb-row" data-index={i}>
              <td class="kb-td kb-td-row">{i + 1}</td>
              <td class="kb-td kb-td-cat">
                <span class="kb-cat-icon">{item.categoryIcon}</span>
                <span>{item.category}</span>
              </td>
              <td class="kb-td kb-td-action">{item.action}</td>
              <td class="kb-td kb-td-keys">
                <span class="kb-keys-inline">{item.keys}</span>
              </td>
              <td class="kb-td kb-td-command"><code>{item.command}</code></td>
              <td class="kb-td kb-td-when">
                {item.when ? item.when.map((w) => (
                  <span class="kb-badge">{w}</span>
                )) : <span class="kb-empty">—</span>}
              </td>
              <td class="kb-td kb-td-notes">
                {item.notes ? <span class="kb-note-text">{item.notes}</span> : <span class="kb-empty">—</span>}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

    <!-- YAML View -->
    <div class="kb-yaml-wrap" id="kb-yaml-view" style="display:none">
      <div class="kb-yaml-content" id="kb-yaml-content">
        {keybindings.map((cat) => (
          <div class="kb-yaml-category" data-cat-id={cat.id}>
            <div class="kb-yaml-line kb-yaml-comment">
              <span class="kb-ln"></span>
              <span class="kb-syn-comment"># ── {cat.label} ──────────────────────────────────</span>
            </div>
            {cat.items.map((item) => {
              const lines = [];
              return (
                <div class="kb-yaml-entry" data-action={item.action} data-keys={item.keys} data-command={item.command}>
                  <div class="kb-yaml-line">
                    <span class="kb-ln"></span>
                    <span class="kb-syn-punctuation">-</span> <span class="kb-syn-key">action</span><span class="kb-syn-punctuation">:</span> <span class="kb-syn-string">"{item.action}"</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="kb-yaml-line">
                    <span class="kb-ln"></span>
                    <span>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="kb-syn-key">keys</span><span class="kb-syn-punctuation">:</span> <span class="kb-syn-string">"{item.keys}"</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="kb-yaml-line">
                    <span class="kb-ln"></span>
                    <span>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="kb-syn-key">command</span><span class="kb-syn-punctuation">:</span> <span class="kb-syn-value">{item.command}</span>
                  </div>
                  {item.when && (
                    <div class="kb-yaml-line">
                      <span class="kb-ln"></span>
                      <span>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="kb-syn-key">when</span><span class="kb-syn-punctuation">:</span> <span class="kb-syn-punctuation">[</span><span class="kb-syn-string">{item.when.map((w, i) => `\"${w}\"${i < item.when!.length - 1 ? ', ' : ''}`).join('')}</span><span class="kb-syn-punctuation">]</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {item.notes && (
                    <div class="kb-yaml-line">
                      <span class="kb-ln"></span>
                      <span>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span><span class="kb-syn-key">notes</span><span class="kb-syn-punctuation">:</span> <span class="kb-syn-comment"># {item.notes}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div class="kb-yaml-line kb-yaml-blank"><span class="kb-ln"></span></div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Status Bar -->
    <footer class="kb-statusbar">
      <span class="kb-status-left" id="kb-status-left">
        {allItems.length} keybindings · {keybindings.length} categories
      </span>
      <span class="kb-status-right" id="kb-status-right">
        Table View
      </span>
    </footer>
  </div>
</SiteLayout>

<script>
  const page = document.querySelector('.kb-page') as HTMLElement;
  const data = JSON.parse(page.dataset.keybindings || '[]');
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('kb-search') as HTMLInputElement;
  const tableView = document.getElementById('kb-table-view')!;
  const yamlView = document.getElementById('kb-yaml-view')!;
  const tbody = document.getElementById('kb-tbody')!;
  const yamlContent = document.getElementById('kb-yaml-content')!;
  const statusLeft = document.getElementById('kb-status-left')!;
  const statusRight = document.getElementById('kb-status-right')!;

  // Flatten for searching
  const allItems = data.flatMap((cat: any) =>
    cat.items.map((item: any) => ({ ...item, category: cat.label }))
  );

  let currentView: 'table' | 'yaml' = 'table';

  // View toggle
  document.querySelectorAll('.kb-toggle-btn').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const view = (btn as HTMLElement).dataset.view as 'table' | 'yaml';
      if (view === currentView) return;
      currentView = view;

      document.querySelectorAll('.kb-toggle-btn').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');

      tableView.style.display = view === 'table' ? '' : 'none';
      yamlView.style.display = view === 'yaml' ? '' : 'none';
      statusRight.textContent = view === 'table' ? 'Table View' : 'YAML View';

      applyFilter(searchInput.value);
    });
  });

  // Search
  searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
    applyFilter(searchInput.value);
  });

  function applyFilter(query: string) {
    const q = query.toLowerCase().trim();
    let visibleCount = 0;

    if (currentView === 'table') {
      const rows = tbody.querySelectorAll('.kb-row');
      rows.forEach((row) => {
        const text = (row as HTMLElement).textContent?.toLowerCase() || '';
        const match = !q || text.includes(q);
        (row as HTMLElement).style.display = match ? '' : 'none';
        if (match) visibleCount++;

        // Re-number visible rows
      });

      // Re-number
      let num = 1;
      rows.forEach((row) => {
        if ((row as HTMLElement).style.display !== 'none') {
          const numCell = row.querySelector('.kb-td-row');
          if (numCell) numCell.textContent = String(num++);
        }
      });
    } else {
      // YAML view filtering
      const entries = yamlContent.querySelectorAll('.kb-yaml-entry');
      entries.forEach((entry) => {
        const el = entry as HTMLElement;
        const text = el.textContent?.toLowerCase() || '';
        const match = !q || text.includes(q);
        el.style.display = match ? '' : 'none';
        if (match) visibleCount++;
      });

      // Show/hide category headers
      yamlContent.querySelectorAll('.kb-yaml-category').forEach((cat) => {
        const entries = cat.querySelectorAll('.kb-yaml-entry');
        const anyVisible = Array.from(entries).some((e) => (e as HTMLElement).style.display !== 'none');
        (cat as HTMLElement).style.display = anyVisible ? '' : 'none';
      });

      // Re-number yaml lines
      let lineNum = 1;
      yamlContent.querySelectorAll('.kb-yaml-category').forEach((cat) => {
        if ((cat as HTMLElement).style.display === 'none') return;
        cat.querySelectorAll('.kb-ln').forEach((ln) => {
          const line = ln.closest('.kb-yaml-line') as HTMLElement;
          const entry = ln.closest('.kb-yaml-entry') as HTMLElement;
          if (entry && entry.style.display === 'none') return;
          ln.textContent = String(lineNum++);
        });
      });
    }

    if (!q) visibleCount = allItems.length;
    const catCount = data.length;
    statusLeft.textContent = q
      ? `${visibleCount} of ${allItems.length} keybindings · \"${q}\"`
      : `${allItems.length} keybindings · ${catCount} categories`;
  }

  // Init line numbers
  applyFilter('');
</script>

export interface ThemeData {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  author: string;
  description: string;
  fileName: string;
  isLight: boolean;
  colors: {
    bg: string;
    fg: string;

    gutterBg: string;
    gutterFg: string;
    sidebarBg: string;

    keyword: string;
    function: string;
    string: string;
    number: string;
    comment: string;
    type: string;
    variable: string;
    operator: string;
    constant: string;
    tag: string;
    attribute: string;
  };
  yaml: string; // The raw YAML content for display
}
```

- **Editor Pane**: Live code preview (Rust sample) that updates CSS variables dynamically based on the selected theme.
- **Detail Pane (320px)**: Theme metadata and color swatches for UI and Syntax colors.
